**[00:29:02.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000034)  
Thank you, our Committee Secretary. At this juncture, the Chair would like to give his opening statement. Today, we take up the proposed 2027 budget of the Commission Audit, our Constitutional Guardian of Public Funds.

**[00:29:18.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000035)  
At a time when public confidence in government spending is being tested, COA must be equipped to detect wrongdoing early, verify that projects exist, and work as intended.

**[00:29:32.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000036)  
And hold accountable those who misuse public money. We also want to recognize the men and women of COA who carry out this difficult mandate every day. Their work is often difficult, technical, and done away from public attention.

**[00:29:47.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000037)  
Audit observations and notices of disallowance are not always well received, but are part of the checks and balances that protect public funds. Government agencies should see them not as an attack, but as

**[00:30:00.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000038)  
an opportunity to explain, correct deficiencies, strengthen internal controls, and improve

**[00:30:05.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000039)  
the use of public funds.

**[00:30:07.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000040)  
We want to know whether the funding level under 2027 NEP is enough for the job we expect

**[00:30:13.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000041)  
COA to perform.

**[00:30:14.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000042)  
We will look closely at your capacity for fraud and performance audits, field inspections,

**[00:30:21.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000043)  
information system audits, citizen participation, staffing, and the timely resolution of audit

**[00:30:28.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000044)  
findings.

**[00:30:28.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000045)  
At the same time, accountability must begin at home.

**[00:30:32.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000046)  
COA itself must meet the same standards of sound financial management, transparency,

**[00:30:37.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000047)  
and internal control that it requires from every government agency.

**[00:30:40.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000048)  
Our objective is simple.

**[00:30:42.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000049)  
Every peso we provide to COA should help protect many more pesos from waste, irregularity,

**[00:30:48.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000050)  
and corruption.

**[00:30:51.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000051)  
At this point, since our colleagues are not yet here, we'll just ask them for their

**[00:30:56.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000052)  
manifestations and their queries later.

**[00:30:58.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000053)  
So, may we request the Commission on Audit to brief us on the proposed fiscal year 2027

**[00:31:06.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000054)  
National Expenditure Program.

**[00:31:08.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000055)  
I ask the indulgence of the others to allow the Commission to present and complete their

**[00:31:14.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000056)  
presentation.

**[00:57:53.350] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000626)

**[00:57:54.930] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000627)

**[00:57:57.050] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000628)  
COA has

**[00:57:57.830] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000629)  
13,378 authorized

**[00:58:00.850] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000630)

**[00:58:03.010] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000631)

**[00:58:05.050] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000632)  
8,693 positions

**[00:58:07.010] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000633)

**[00:58:10.470] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000635)

**[00:58:12.790] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000636)

**[00:58:14.990] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000637)

**[00:58:17.330] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000638)

**[00:58:21.490] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000640)

**[00:58:22.670] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000641)

**[00:58:24.730] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000642)

**[00:58:26.910] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000643)

**[00:58:31.810] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000645)  
So, ito po.

**[00:58:33.010] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000646)

**[00:58:34.930] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000647)

**[00:58:36.810] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000648)  
positions vacant po tayo.

**[00:58:40.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000649)

**[00:58:41.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000650)  
During the budget hearing at the House,

**[00:58:43.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000651)

**[00:58:45.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000652)

**[00:58:47.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000653)

**[00:58:50.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000654)

**[00:58:52.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000655)  
More flexible work arrangements, transfers

**[00:58:54.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000656)

**[00:58:56.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000657)

**[00:58:57.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000658)

**[00:58:59.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000659)

**[00:59:20.440] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000669)

**[00:59:22.260] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000670)

**[00:59:24.960] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000671)

**[00:59:26.760] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000672)

**[00:59:28.780] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000673)

4,544

**[00:59:34.320] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000676)

**[00:59:36.340] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000677)

82%

**[00:59:43.000] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000680)  
So, notably po,

**[00:59:44.940] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000681)  
ito po yung parang

**[00:59:46.440] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000682)  
based on the system ranking of positions po,

**[00:59:49.400] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000683)  
meron pong category ito.

May entry level ng

**[00:59:52.640] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000685)  
state auditing examiner

**[00:59:54.840] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000686)  
examiner 2, which is the

**[00:59:56.600] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000687)  
entry level. Then,

**[00:59:58.680] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000688)  
after a year,

**[01:00:06.660] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000690)  
So based on our records, the State Auditing Examiner 2 out of the unfilled positions

**[01:00:13.560] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000691)

**[01:00:30.060] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000694)

**[01:00:33.060] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000695)

**[01:00:39.940] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000696)  
this can be promoted na po sa State Auditor 1,

**[01:00:43.580] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000697)  
and after, we have to follow po yung system ranking of positions.

**[01:00:49.140] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000698)  
So we're doing that po.

**[01:00:51.140] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000699)  
So it will take, notably po, it will take,

**[01:00:53.980] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000700)  
a span of months because of the compliance with the administrative rules of the Civil Service po.

**[01:01:02.200] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000701)  
So, but we, for the past years po, for the last three years po,

**[01:01:07.480] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000702)

**[01:01:12.120] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000703)

**[01:01:18.440] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000704)

**[01:01:20.260] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000705)

**[01:01:23.980] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000706)

**[01:01:34.840] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-000708)

**[01:01:41.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000709)

**[01:01:57.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000713)

**[01:02:03.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000714)

**[01:02:08.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000715)

**[01:02:10.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000716)

**[01:02:23.980] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000720)

**[01:02:27.260] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000721)

**[01:02:31.960] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000722)

**[01:02:40.300] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000723)

**[01:02:44.040] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000724)

**[01:02:51.620] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000725)

**[01:02:53.420] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000726)  
And then,

**[01:02:53.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000727)

**[01:03:03.620] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000728)

**[01:03:05.280] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000729)

**[01:03:10.500] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000730)

**[01:03:19.600] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-000731)

**[01:03:27.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000732)

**[01:03:30.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000733)

**[01:47:26.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002130)

**[01:47:29.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002131)

**[01:47:39.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002132)

**[01:48:00.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002135)

**[01:48:17.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002137)

**[01:48:26.700] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002139)

**[01:48:33.860] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002140)

**[01:48:40.040] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002141)

**[01:48:56.560] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002144)

**[01:49:00.860] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002145)

**[01:49:02.660] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002146)

**[01:49:04.700] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002147)

**[01:49:08.660] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002148)

**[01:49:11.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002149)

**[01:49:31.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002152)

**[01:49:45.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002154)

**[01:49:50.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002155)

**[01:49:51.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002156)

**[01:49:51.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002157)

**[01:51:01.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002173)

**[01:51:07.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002174)

**[01:51:09.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002175)  
RDC approval, program of works, tapos basta po nasa flood-prone, top 10 flood-prone provinces

**[01:51:21.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002177)

**[01:51:28.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002178)

**[01:51:36.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002179)

**[01:51:37.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002180)

**[01:52:49.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002196)

**[01:52:55.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002198)

**[01:53:02.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002200)

**[01:53:04.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002201)

**[01:53:06.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002202)

**[01:53:13.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002203)

**[01:53:17.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002204)

**[01:54:08.700] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002216)

**[01:54:15.900] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002217)  
Literally, figuratively, physically, mentally, and sometimes spiritually.

**[01:54:21.260] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002218)  
Mr. Chairman, just to put on record, Mr. Chairman, and I'd like to give this information to our COA family headed by Chairman Cordoba.

**[01:54:36.080] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002219)  
Yung po sa amin sa Bulacan, for example, doon sa Paumbong, sa Paumbong, sa Barangay Cruz,

**[01:54:43.040] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002220)  
tsaka Barangay Masukol, eh hindi na po baha yung problema nila.

**[01:54:51.350] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002221)

**[01:54:53.530] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002222)

**[01:55:00.450] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002223)

**[01:55:07.450] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002224)

**[01:55:14.590] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002225)  
At unfortunately, sabi nga nila, hindi na yung baha problema namin.

**[01:55:19.230] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002226)  
Kasi kinakain na ng lupa, mabuburan na kami sa mapa.

**[01:55:21.830] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002227)  
Ganon din po yung kalagayan sa Hagonoy, Bulacan, sa Barangay Pugad at Barangay Tibagin.

**[01:55:28.930] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002228)  
Ano naman yung nangyari dito sa dalawang barangay na ito?

**[01:55:32.030] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002229)  
Before the fiasco, before this erupted, the kasuklam-suklam na mga pangyayari about flood control,

**[01:55:40.450] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002230)

**[01:55:48.450] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002231)

**[01:55:49.390] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002232)  
Pero dahil gagawin at palalakihin, patitibayin, i-strengthenin yung seawall, binakbak na po nila.

**[01:55:58.470] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002233)  
Sisimulan na eh, gagawin na po.

**[01:56:00.630] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002234)  
Nung gagawin na po, natigil lahat ngayon ng flood control.

**[01:56:04.970] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002235)  
So ano nangyari?

**[01:56:06.350] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002236)

**[01:56:19.030] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002237)  
Talagang ako, fight ako sa firing squad eh, sa mga taong ganyan eh.

**[01:56:26.610] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002238)  
Eh, hindi po ginawa.

**[01:56:28.390] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002239)

**[01:56:37.830] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002240)

**[01:56:41.810] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002241)

**[01:56:47.150] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002242)  
at barangay...

**[01:56:48.350] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002243)

**[01:56:52.890] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002244)  
sa Hagunoy, barangay Pugat, barangay Tibagin, sa Hagunoy.

**[01:56:56.730] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002245)  
Eh, hindi na po problema nila yung ba.

**[01:56:58.330] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002246)  
Ang problema nila, mawawala na po sa mapa yung kanilang mga barangay.

**[01:57:03.090] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002247)

**[01:57:06.930] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002248)  
na I have to call the attention of the DPWH Secretary because

**[01:57:12.310] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002249)

**[01:57:16.070] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002250)

**[01:57:17.270] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002251)  
In Hagunoy, meron naman po doon kasama doon sa problema na may kaso.

**[01:57:26.370] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002252)

**[01:57:29.510] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002253)

**[01:57:32.470] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002254)

**[01:57:38.230] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002255)

**[01:57:45.410] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002256)  
dahil ngayon pag nakialam,

**[01:57:47.270] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002257)

**[01:57:50.130] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002258)

**[01:57:56.550] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002259)

**[01:58:01.870] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002260)

**[01:58:04.990] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002261)

**[01:58:06.790] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002262)

**[01:58:10.150] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002263)  
Dahil lang daw po may kaso, hindi po ginawa.

**[01:58:12.510] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002264)  
So, nag-suffer yung buong barangay.

**[01:58:17.270] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002265)  
Yun lang po yung mga maliliit na bagay na gano'n.

**[01:58:19.990] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002266)  
And I hope, ang COA naman natin, hindi naman in the box yan.

**[01:58:23.750] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002267)  
I don't think yung COA natin, extremist yung mga COA auditors natin

**[01:58:28.170] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002268)  
para yung mga ganitong bagay na ito, hindi matulungan yung mga kababayan po natin.

**[01:58:36.570] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002269)  
So, yun lang po, Mr. Chairman, gusto kong ipaabot sa ating mga kasama sa COA

**[01:58:41.750] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002270)  
na malaking tulong ang kailangan ng mga kababayan natin.

**[01:58:46.430] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002271)  
Lalo na po doon sa mga apektado ng baha.

**[01:58:51.530] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002272)  
Dahil nakakasunog ang apoy, hindi na po tayo magluluto at papatayin natin sa gutom yung ating mga kababayan.

**[01:58:59.130] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002273)  
I'm really not happy na wala hong flood control the past year program kahit na ito'y nararapat.

**[01:59:07.370] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002274)  
And records will bail me out.

**[01:59:09.590] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002275)  
Hindi ako sumuporta sa mga flood control programs because hindi ko nakikita na ito'y integrated

**[01:59:14.650] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002276)  
at may master plan.

**[01:59:16.430] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002277)  
That's the only reason.

**[01:59:17.490] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002278)  
And these committees in the Senate, mga senador nakasama ko at mga nakatrabaho ko,

**[01:59:25.870] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002279)  
alam po yung dahilan kung bakit ganito yung aking adhikain dito sa usapin nito.

**[01:59:31.530] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002280)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Chair.

**[01:59:33.130] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002281)  
Thank you, Senator Villanueva.

**[01:59:35.270] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002282)  
Actually, sa totoo lang, that's why we wanted also to resume the flood control projects.

**[01:59:42.370] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002283)  
The uncompleted one, that is.

**[01:59:43.710] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002284)  
Because we have seen that...

**[01:59:46.430] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002285)  
Dito sa monsoon rains, wala pa ngang bagyo ito.

**[01:59:49.710] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002286)  
Tuloy-tuloy na ulan, lubog ang Metro Manila, lubog ang Bulacan, lubog ang Pampanga, hanggang Tarlac.

**[01:59:55.770] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002287)  
So, ano eh.

**[01:59:57.350] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002288)  
Well, iba na rin talaga yung climate change.

**[02:00:00.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002289)  
But still, we need to resume the flood-mitigating projects, especially the complete, the uncompleted.

**[02:00:09.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002290)  
Siguro wala namang masama roon, Chairman, di ba, as long as these projects are not found to be irregular or substandard.

**[02:00:17.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002291)  
I think this will be more beneficial as it would prevent the further suffering of the people in Bulacan and Pampanga and even Metro Manila and Tarlac.

**[02:00:27.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002292)  
At least kung ma-mitigate natin, mabawasan natin yung pagbabaha, masara natin yung gaps, kasi mas masama yung may puwang eh.

**[02:00:36.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002293)  
Tubig yan. Maghahanap talaga ng papasukan yan.

**[02:00:38.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002294)  
Tapos pinipigilan mo namang mawala yung tubig para mawala yung baha.

**[02:00:42.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002295)  
So parang naging ano siya.

**[02:00:45.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002296)  
So ang ano natin is to close the gaps.

**[02:00:48.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002297)  
And also, if I would, magsimula tayo, Senator Villanueva, yung NIA and DPWH.

**[02:00:55.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002298)  
Dapat dito,

**[02:00:56.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002299)  
in the absence of a master plan, convergence na.

**[02:01:00.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002300)  
And then sa taas pa lang dapat meron nang water impounding.

**[02:01:05.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002301)  
Tapos nakaano yan ng flood control, water impounding.

**[02:01:10.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002302)  
Kasi pag tag-ulan, sobra-sobra ulan natin.

**[02:01:13.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002303)  
Pag tagtuyot naman, walang-wala tayo, di ba?

**[02:01:15.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002304)  
So I think we should change yung view, yung view eh na yung tubig hindi dapat perwisyo.

**[02:01:22.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002305)  
Dapat treated as a resource.

**[02:01:24.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002306)  
Pero gawin natin.

**[02:01:26.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002307)  
Kailangan natin ng tamang flood water management, I think.

**[02:01:29.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002308)  
Yun ang atin pong direksyon, di ba?

**[02:01:33.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002309)  
Sige, Chairperson Cordoba.

**[02:01:35.060] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002310)  
Thank you for, salamat po sa lahat ng binanggit ninyo.

**[02:01:37.740] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002311)  
Actually po, Your Honors, if may mga kailangan po na i-cover sila DPWH,

**[02:01:42.940] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002312)  
kagaya po nung binabanggit ninyo, kasi may kaso, hindi pwedeng gawin.

**[02:01:45.400] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002313)  
Kung kailangan po, we're willing to sit down on an item per item with the DPWH.

**[02:01:51.640] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002314)  
And actually, we're doing it po.

**[02:01:53.400] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002315)  
Pagka po may mga question sila,

**[02:01:54.860] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002316)  
tinutulungan namin sila para

**[02:01:56.320] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002317)  
magawa po nila kahit po may mga pending na kaso, pending na mga legalities.

**[02:02:04.660] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002318)  
But importante po yung public service ay tuloy-tuloy.

**[02:02:07.540] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002319)  
Hindi po siya dapat huminto.

**[02:02:08.980] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002320)  
Salamat po.

**[02:02:10.920] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002321)  
One last issue.

**[02:02:12.880] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002322)  
If there's nothing more about the flood control program.

**[02:02:17.040] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002323)  
Tinitingnan ko lang po kasi itong COA's Technical Services and Systems Audit Subprogram

**[02:02:23.280] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002324)  
that targets only 18 ISIT audit or

**[02:02:26.200] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002325)  
evaluation reports in 2027. Is that level of coverage sufficient given government's accelerating digitalization

**[02:02:37.520] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002326)  
and growing cybersecurity and information system risk?

**[02:02:42.920] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002327)  
Nakita ko lang ho yung 2027 target, 18 ISIT audit or evaluation reports.

**[02:02:51.920] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002328)  
Sandali lang po.

**[02:02:53.160] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002329)  
Sige, please.

**[02:02:53.960] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002330)  
Yes, sorry.

**[02:02:57.640] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002331)  
We just want to be clarified on this, how it prioritizes systems for audit and whether existing personnel

**[02:03:06.600] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002332)  
and technology ay sufficient po dito to cover yung high-risk government information systems?

**[02:03:15.060] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002333)  
Thank you for the question, Mr. Chair, Sen. Villanueva.

**[02:03:18.720] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002334)  
Si Ascom Isidro po ang sasagot po.

**[02:03:22.440] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002335)  
Thank you.

**[02:03:22.760] COA Assistant Commissioner Lucila M. Isidro** (asr-002336)  
Good afternoon, Mr. Chair and Sen. Villanueva.

**[02:03:26.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002337)  
Please proceed.

**[02:03:27.640] COA Assistant Commissioner Lucila M. Isidro** (asr-002338)  
We have an office called the Internal Information Technology Audit Office under the Systems and Technical Services sector that does the IS audit and IT technical evaluation of procurement contracts and other contracts.

**[02:03:54.350] COA Assistant Commissioner Lucila M. Isidro** (asr-002339)  
At present, we have only 64 warm bodies.

**[02:04:00.550] COA Assistant Commissioner Lucila M. Isidro** (asr-002340)  
But ideally, the office needs 164 manpower with an existing approved plantelia of 81 plantelia positions.

**[02:04:12.910] COA Assistant Commissioner Lucila M. Isidro** (asr-002341)  
And these 64 warm bodies consist of auditors and administrative personnel, including...

**[02:04:29.510] COA Assistant Commissioner Lucila M. Isidro** (asr-002342)  
We also have...

**[02:04:32.150] COA Assistant Commissioner Lucila M. Isidro** (asr-002343)  
Certified Information Systems Auditor.

**[02:04:35.510] COA Assistant Commissioner Lucila M. Isidro** (asr-002344)  
We have Public Procurement Specialist, Certified Fraud Examiner, Certified Public Accountants, and other disciplines consisting the office.

**[02:04:51.190] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002345)  
So ito, magandang problema yata ito, kasi you have 64 but you have 81 available.

**[02:05:00.470] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002346)  
Tama ba yung pagkakaintindi ko, ma?am?

**[02:05:02.150] COA Assistant Commissioner Lucila M. Isidro** (asr-002347)  
Yes po.

**[02:05:02.930] COA Assistant Commissioner Lucila M. Isidro** (asr-002348)  
Yes po.

**[02:05:03.430] COA Assistant Commissioner Lucila M. Isidro** (asr-002349)  
Per our record, ma?am.

**[02:05:04.530] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002350)  
Pero hindi ninyo ma-fill up at the same problem that you are talking about?

**[02:05:10.690] COA Assistant Commissioner Lucila M. Isidro** (asr-002351)  
For the Information Systems Auditors, there is a certain criteria that need to be satisfied.

**[02:05:18.390] COA Assistant Commissioner Lucila M. Isidro** (asr-002352)  
Like, they need to be...

**[02:05:20.130] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002353)  
I'm sure. I was the head of agency before.

**[02:05:23.530] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002354)  
So hindi ma-satisfy yun, kaya hindi ma-fill up?

**[02:05:26.810] COA Assistant Commissioner Lucila M. Isidro** (asr-002355)  
At present po, hindi po ma-satisfy po.

**[02:05:29.670] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002356)  
Yun yung dahilan kung bakit

**[02:05:31.990] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002357)  
ko tinatanong yung questions ko kanina.

**[02:05:34.110] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002358)  
Is there a way that we can somehow adjust?

**[02:05:39.430] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002359)  
Yun talaga kasi yung kailangan?

**[02:05:41.890] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002360)  
At wala talaga tayong makikita?

**[02:05:45.070] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002361)  
Hahanap ba tayo sa Milky Way Galaxy?

**[02:05:49.010] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002362)  
Mag-train tayo sa TESDA?

**[02:05:52.810] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-002363)  
Your Honor, it's really...

**[02:05:58.030] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-002364)  
We are...

**[02:05:58.950] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-002365)  
We are...

**[02:05:59.270] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-002366)  
Dependent po kasi talagang sa compliance ng administrative rules ng civil service.

**[02:06:07.730] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-002367)  
So, kaya nga po kasi nasa standardization law po kami under.

**[02:06:13.670] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-002368)  
So, we really need to follow yung system ranking oppositions.

**[02:06:18.990] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-002369)  
So, hindi po kami ka...

**[02:06:20.710] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-002370)  
Yung higher levels po kasi, for example,

**[02:06:23.750] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-002371)  
as yung entry level is so low,

**[02:06:26.350] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-002372)  
pero you cannot hire po.

**[02:06:29.270] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-002373)  
Maglulundag ka ng salary grades kasi that is against dun sa administrative rules po ng civil service.

**[02:06:38.430] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002374)  
And that will be addressed dun sa binabanggit ni Chairman na panukalang batas na ipupush po namin.

**[02:06:46.370] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-002375)  
Yes po.

**[02:06:47.030] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-002376)  
If we are to be exempted sa standardization.

**[02:06:48.550] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002377)  
Because otherwise, kahit ilang Pasko yung dumating, hindi ninyo ma-fill up-an.

**[02:06:54.890] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-002378)  
Yes, even po na meron ka talagang qualified.

**[02:06:57.990] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-002379)  
Yan yung mga hinaharap.

**[02:06:59.270] COA Assistant Commissioner Lorna Cabochan** (asr-002380)  
At the bottom, hindi ko po makakapag more than three salary grades.

**[02:07:04.790] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002381)  
I got that. I know where you're coming from.

**[02:07:07.470] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002382)  
I experienced that personally when I was with TESDA.

**[02:07:11.470] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002383)  
Anyway, one last.

**[02:07:14.050] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002384)  
Yung financial management, nakita ko lang COA's own 2025 audit.

**[02:07:20.710] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002385)  
You identified accounting deficiencies involving approximately 5.1 billion pesos.

**[02:07:25.870] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002386)  
What accounts for the scale of...

**[02:07:29.270] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002387)  
of these deficiencies, and what corrective measures?

**[02:07:33.010] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002388)  
May independent audit eh, in-identify various accounting deficiencies totaling 5.1 billion.

**[02:07:40.550] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002389)  
The amount does not by itself mean funds siguro that were lost or misused.

**[02:07:46.670] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002390)  
But it is significant enough to perhaps get an explanation what this is.

**[02:08:06.230] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002391)  
I got this from our LBRMO.

**[02:08:10.250] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002392)  
Sa page 17, number 4.

**[02:08:15.790] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002393)  
Various accounting deficiencies noted amounted to 5.101 billion as at year-end.

**[02:08:27.390] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002394)  
LBRMO. Dito po, sa Senado po ito.

**[02:08:30.930] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002395)  
Kaya nung nakita ko, sabi ko i-raise ko ito eh.

**[02:08:34.490] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002396)  
You're not aware of this?

**[02:08:35.590] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002397)  
Hindi po, your honor.

**[02:08:37.250] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002398)  
Okay.

**[02:08:38.890] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002399)  
Okay, I'll set it.

**[02:08:40.250] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002400)  
This is part of the audit report ng COA.

**[02:08:55.060] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002401)  
Audit observation number 4.

**[02:08:58.520] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002402)  
But anyway, I will just furnish a copy nito.

**[02:09:02.400] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002403)  
Thank you.

**[02:09:02.760] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002404)  
Just wanted to make sure that we'll be able to clarify this.

**[02:09:07.620] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002405)  
Kasi nagulat lang ako dun sa 5 billion eh.

**[02:09:10.160] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002406)  
I'm sure wala namang meaning where they're...

**[02:09:14.820] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002407)  
May misuse or loss of funds.

**[02:09:18.240] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002408)  
It's just that I think we need to explain this further

**[02:09:21.180] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002409)  
para lang maset natin yung high standards ng COA.

**[02:09:26.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002410)  
Salamat, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:09:27.320] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002411)  
That will be all.

**[02:09:28.280] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002412)  
And again, let me put on record my sincerest thanks and gratitude

**[02:09:32.700] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002413)  
to the men and women of COA for your service.

**[02:09:39.040] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002414)  
And most of all, I'd like to put on record my full support

**[02:09:42.740] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002415)  
for the...

**[02:09:44.820] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002416)  
approval of this briefing so that we can proceed

**[02:09:50.840] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002417)  
for plenary action of their budget, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:09:55.740] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002418)  
Thank you.

**[02:09:56.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002419)  
Thank you, Senator Villanueva, for your support

**[02:09:58.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002420)  
to our Commission on Audit.

**[02:10:04.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002421)  
Alam mo, ang hirap ng Commission on Audit,

**[02:10:06.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002422)  
ang tingin sa mga auditor, madalas, kontrabida eh.

**[02:10:10.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002423)  
Parang pag nakikita ito, lalo ng mga LG,

**[02:10:12.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002424)  
nako, ito na naman ito, mga kontrabida.

**[02:10:13.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002425)  
But you know, this...

**[02:10:14.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002426)  
Auditors, state auditors, provide the checks and balances.

**[02:10:19.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002427)  
Make sure that money is well spent in government, diba?

**[02:10:22.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002428)  
Kaya kailangan namin kayo dito, especially in our...

**[02:10:25.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002429)  
in our trust to bring back the confidence of people in government, no?

**[02:10:31.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002430)  
Na napakalaking bagay, lalo na dito sa ating budgeting.

**[02:10:35.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002431)  
Before we end, I would like to ask some questions from our...

**[02:10:38.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002432)  
kasi iniinvolve namin yung civil society organizations, no?

**[02:10:43.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002433)  
So that...

**[02:10:44.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002434)  
Ito mga watchdogs can help us because we cannot...

**[02:10:48.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002435)  
Sabi ko, it's humanly impossible for us to appreciate everything

**[02:10:53.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002436)  
in so short a time during the budget season, no?

**[02:10:57.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002437)  
Gabundok yung ibang volumes yun na pinapadala, line items.

**[02:11:01.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002438)  
So we are involving the civil society organizations, watchdogs,

**[02:11:05.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002439)  
and everybody else.

**[02:11:06.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002440)  
Pag may nakita sila na irregular, to let us know

**[02:11:10.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002441)  
so that we can already confront the agencies, no?

**[02:11:12.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002442)  
So ito po, from the CSO,

**[02:11:14.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002443)  
naging concerned po ng maraming CSOs and groups

**[02:11:17.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002444)  
na nagmo-monitor ng budget process sa mga nakaraang taon

**[02:11:20.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002445)  
ang delays ng publication ng COA's annual audit reports,

**[02:11:25.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002446)  
particularly for the fiscal year 2023

**[02:11:28.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002447)  
at sa ibang agency sa fiscal year 2024.

**[02:11:32.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002448)  
To address this under Section 107 of the 2026 GAA

**[02:11:36.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002449)  
sets an October 31 deadline for the publication of annual audit reports.

**[02:11:42.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002450)  
Ang tanong, kumusta po ang

**[02:11:44.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002451)  
implementation nito? Have all fiscal year 2024

**[02:11:47.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002452)  
annual audit reports already been published on your website?

**[02:11:52.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002453)  
Published na po. Okay. Complete.

**[02:11:55.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002454)  
Ito pong, is it on track to make all 2025 naman po

**[02:11:59.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002455)  
audit reports available by October 31.

**[02:12:02.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002456)  
Okay, so ongoing.

**[02:12:04.620] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002457)  
Idagdag ko lang po, Mr. Chair.

**[02:12:06.200] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002458)  
Kasi nung 2023 for 2024, nakalagay po kasi sa GAA na December,

**[02:12:13.620] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002459)  
end of December po.

**[02:12:14.820] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002460)  
Last day. Kaya po, na-delay din talaga.

**[02:12:17.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002461)  
So ngayon, October 31 na?

**[02:12:18.220] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002462)  
October 31 na po.

**[02:12:19.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002463)  
Sa follow-up, Chairman, would it be feasible for COA

**[02:12:22.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002464)  
to publish the annual audit reports earlier?

**[02:12:25.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002465)  
Ang request nila, July 30,

**[02:12:27.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002466)  
so that Congress will have sufficient time to consider the audit findings.

**[02:12:33.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002467)  
Kasi during the budget process, wala pa yung ano nyo, October 31.

**[02:12:37.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002468)  
So, ang ano lang nila, kung kaya yung July 31,

**[02:12:40.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002469)  
para po meron silang time to consider the audit findings.

**[02:12:44.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002470)  
During the budget process, is it feasible?

**[02:12:49.460] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-002471)  
Sir, pursuant to Section 107 of the GAA 2027,

**[02:12:56.360] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-002472)  
may requirement kasi po, upon the receipt of the annual audit report

**[02:13:01.940] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-002473)  
by the audited agency, required sila to submit the status of actions taken

**[02:13:06.840] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-002474)  
on the audit observations and memorandum.

**[02:13:09.740] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-002475)  
So within 60 days, isasabit nila yan doon sa COA for

**[02:13:14.820] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-002476)  
for the publication together with the annual audit report.

**[02:13:18.120] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-002477)  
So, June 30 ang deadline of the transmittal plus 60.

**[02:13:23.880] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-002478)  
So, talagang, tapos gagawin pa yan ng COA,

**[02:13:26.940] COA Assistant Commissioner Nilda B. Plaras** (asr-002479)  
iko-confirm pa yan, i-evaluate.

**[02:13:29.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002480)  
So, talagang October 31?

**[02:13:33.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002481)  
Sige, Chairman.

**[02:13:34.840] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002482)  
Ang ginagawa naman po namin, as soon as matapos,

**[02:13:36.760] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002483)  
kasi hindi naman po sabay-sabay,

**[02:13:38.380] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002484)  
as soon as matapos, pinapadala po namin agad sa inyo pong office.

**[02:13:43.680] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002485)  
So, may mga hard copies po.

**[02:13:44.820] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002486)  
So, pinapauna na po namin.

**[02:13:45.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002487)  
That would help a lot, especially during the budget process.

**[02:13:48.540] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002488)  
Pinapadala po namin agad.

**[02:13:49.640] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002489)  
Basta po matapos, hindi lang po sabay-sabay.

**[02:13:52.040] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002490)  
As it comes lang po.

**[02:13:54.920] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002491)  
We sent it to...

**[02:13:55.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002492)  
Kinaaano kasi nila concerning availability raw sa public.

**[02:13:58.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002493)  
So, if we can make it earlier nalang siguro, the earliest possible time.

**[02:14:02.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002494)  
Yung kaya lang naman po.

**[02:14:05.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002495)  
COA describes the citizen participatory audit as a mechanism

**[02:14:08.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002496)  
that brings citizens and civil society organizations into the audit process.

**[02:14:12.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002497)  
Nagamit na rin ito natin.

**[02:14:14.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002498)  
In previous audits, including in relation to the flood control master plan sa Kamanawa area.

**[02:14:21.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002499)  
For 2027, COA requested 40.7 million for CPA requirements under Tier 2,

**[02:14:29.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002500)  
but this was not included in the NEP.

**[02:14:33.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002501)  
So, on follow-up, we have also received reports that in the past engagements,

**[02:14:36.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002502)  
that citizen partners may be receiving only 250 pesos in logistical support.

**[02:14:41.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002503)  
Can COA confirm whether this is accurate?

**[02:14:44.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002504)  
Is that 250?

**[02:14:44.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002505)  
Provided per day, proactively, or to the entire audit engagement?

**[02:14:49.980] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002506)  
Na-increase na po yan, Your Honor.

**[02:14:51.800] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002507)  
But actually nga po, nasimulan na po namin yan.

**[02:14:54.040] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002508)  
Yan po binabanggit ko sa inyo kanina,

**[02:14:56.100] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002509)  
na one of the observations po na nakita doon sa citizen participatory

**[02:15:00.000] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002510)  
audit

**[02:15:02.140] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002511)  
ayun pong wala pong master plan.

**[02:15:04.880] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002512)  
So, actually po,

**[02:15:06.120] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002513)

**[02:15:08.040] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002514)

**[02:15:10.240] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002515)

**[02:15:12.160] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002516)

**[02:15:13.980] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002517)  
But hindi po lang sinama

**[02:15:16.000] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002518)  
this year, ah, for next year.

**[02:15:18.820] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002519)  
But, tuloy-tuloy

**[02:15:20.280] COA Chairperson Gamaliel A. Cordoba** (asr-002520)

**[02:15:22.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002521)

**[02:15:23.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002522)  
40 million, 40.7 million

**[02:15:26.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002523)

**[02:15:28.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002524)

**[02:15:30.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002525)
